# PING Social — Facebook engagement log

## RESUMED (2026-09-30 ~16:05 MDT, Nolan's binding order; supersedes the 2026-09-30 ~14:07 freeze)
Nolan: do not freeze socials. Start again. Standing rule: NEVER freeze engagement on prose/policy grounds; scale back ONLY on a platform flag (checkpoint, warning, action block, restriction). A failed pass is retried until the objective is met (2026-09-26 no-scale-back order + 2026-09-30 ~13:49 rerun-to-objective order still in force). Never pad with ineligible content; never violate never-reengage, geography, or quality rules to fill caps; if the pool is structurally exhausted, report the exact shortfall and the decisions that would unlock more. Stay silent on routine passes; surface only flags, warnings, owner replies, real mistakes, or genuine decisions.
- TikTok is NOT covered by this resume: it is scaled back because it was flagged (restriction banner, tripwire HOLD, cron disabled 2026-09-29). Per Nolan's rule it stays held until the flag clears, not on a timer.

MECHANICAL ENFORCEMENT (2026-09-14): the never-re-engage rule (every Page/post ever handled is off-limits) is enforced by ~/workspace/outreach/engagement-guard.py, run inside ~/workspace/pig-fb/run_pass.sh on every job file before anything reaches the Pig. Blocked actions are stripped with a GUARD audit line below; prose alone is not the enforcement.
EXECUTABLE HOLD STATE (2026-09-30): the only freeze/hold mechanism is ~/workspace/outreach/holds.json, read by authority-check.py FIRST (step 0) at both build time (cron body runs the gate before discovery/job build) and dispatch time (run_pass.sh runs the gate before anything reaches the Pig). set-hold.py is the only writer (owner binding order or observed platform flag, provenance mandatory, audit in hold-changes.jsonl). Prose in this header NEVER holds a surface; it is documentation only.
## CEO MODE RAMP SCHEDULE (2026-09-23, Nolan's binding order; supersedes all older volume notes)
Facebook drops to ALMOST NOTHING and ramps back only on proof.
- Stage 0 (NOW, effective 2026-09-23 ~13:05 MDT): up to 5 likes per pass, 0 comments, 3h cadence. (Pass 102, dispatched ~11:36 MDT and in flight under the old caps, completes normally; Stage 0 applies from pass 103.)
- Stage 1: 10 likes + 2 comments per pass, after 7 consecutive clean passes (no guard blocks, no warnings, no checkpoints).
- Stage 2: 20 likes + 5 comments per pass, after another 7 consecutive clean passes.
- Stage 3: 40 likes + 10 comments per pass, after another 7 consecutive clean passes.
- Stage 4: resume heavy ceiling (75 likes + 20 comments) only after a full 4 weeks clean total.
Rules: at most one stage increase per week. Any checkpoint, warning, or restriction anywhere on Facebook drops the stage immediately to Stage 0 with a 14-day hold before Stage 1 can restart; the affected pass ends with zero further engagement and no retries.

Current caps: **75 likes + 20 comments per pass** (2-DAY MAX SPRINT, Nolan's binding order 2026-09-23 ~18:10 MDT, through 2026-09-25; then revert to the CEO MODE weekly ramp). 14-day hold active: no. Any checkpoint, warning, or restriction drops to 5 likes + 0 comments per pass with a 14-day hold before the weekly ramp can restart. (The 2026-09-23 ~13:52 accelerated 20+10 schedule is superseded for the sprint duration.)

RERUN-TO-OBJECTIVE (2026-09-30 ~13:49 MDT, Nolan's standing order): if a wave does not meet the 75+20 objective, rerun the wave until it does. A below-cap pass is a failed attempt, not a completed pass. Do not log "honest below-cap" as done and wait for the next schedule. Keep running discovery-build-curate-dispatch cycles. Never pad with ineligible content and never violate the never-reengage, geography, or quality rules to fill the gap; if the pool is structurally exhausted, report the exact shortfall and the decisions that would unlock more.

Historical caps note (superseded): Nolan's 2026-09-15 heavy directive was up to 75 likes + 20 comments per pass, every 3h; 25 likes + 7 comments reduced caps stayed in force through end of 2026-09-15 MDT (trust rebuild after the 2026-09-13/14 lock); the heavy ceiling resumed 2026-09-16 and ran until the 2026-09-23 CEO MODE directive.

First live Facebook engagement pass from the Pig's always-on headed Chrome (CDP :9223). Cloud brain discovered via facebook-cli; Pig hands executed.

Job (3 actions, 60-120s pacing): 2 likes + 1 comment on group posts in group 2803047646497352.

Results:
- LIKE https://www.facebook.com/groups/2803047646497352/permalink/4065704236898347/ (Kenny's Overhead Doors post): pass run failed ("like button not found"); recovered during selector fix verification via probe (DOM click on topmost Like button; "Like: 1 person" count appeared). Counts as completed.
- LIKE https://www.facebook.com/groups/2803047646497352/permalink/4066988156769955/ (Abbotts Cleanup & Restoration post): pass run failed ("like button not found"); completed on re-run with fixed worker ("like registered (button state changed)").
- COMMENT https://www.facebook.com/groups/2803047646497352/permalink/4066988156769955/: posted OK ("comment text visible"). Text: "Assess, strategize, dry. The demolition free playbook. Mold never stood a chance."

Effective total: 3/3 actions landed. No checkpoints, no action blocks, no verification challenges. Login held throughout.

Worker bugs found and fixed in ~/workspace/pig-fb/engage.js (synced to Pig):
1. engage.js never called process.exit on the success path, so the node process lingered forever holding a CDP connection (stale dryrun worker had to be killed). Added process.exit(0).
2. doLike scoped the Like-button search to the first div[role="article"], but group permalink pages render the post action bar outside any article and stack suggested posts below. Now picks the topmost visible [aria-label="Like"] on the page.
3. Playwright's click() times out on FB like buttons (never "stable"); now uses DOM .click() via evaluate.
4. Group-post Like buttons carry no aria-pressed; verification now watches for button state change (Unlike appears, Like button disappears, or "Like: N people" count appears/increments).

Infra note: the cron worker's backgrounded run_pass.sh died with its context before the pass finished, leaving the job file with no worker. Recovery was manual this time. If this recurs, make run_pass.sh launches persistent (nohup) or have the worker re-check the inbox for orphaned jobs.

## Pass 90 IN FLIGHT — fb-20260922-024222 (dispatched 2026-09-21 20:42 MDT; pass id is UTC-derived)

Correction of the record (verified 2026-09-21 ~20:50 MDT against the Pig and the intent ledger): the backgrounded run_pass.sh from ~20:35 MDT did NOT die before dispatching. The earlier "attempt 1 FAILED" framing was inferred from missing local evidence and was wrong. What actually happened: guard filtered 108 to 92 eligible / 16 blocked at 20:42:27 MDT; 92 intents were written and marked EXECUTION at 20:42:28; the job landed in the Pig inbox at 20:42:37; the engage.js worker launched at 20:42:39 (PID 18508, command line confirmed via Win32_Process) and is executing. What died was only the LOCAL run_pass.sh wrapper when the scratch /tmp tmpfs was wiped mid-turn (job file, background session, and /tmp/fb-pass90 scan files all vanished). The Pig-side worker is independent and kept running. The so-called "attempt 2" (~20:47 MDT foreground run) dispatched nothing: all 92 eligible actions were refused as UNKNOWN_EXECUTION duplicates of the in-flight pass. The intent guard worked exactly as designed, no duplicate engagement launched. Attempt-2 result: {"ok":false,"guard":"all_actions_blocked","eligible":0,"blocked":16,"intent_refused":92}.

Guard blocks (16): 3 page-slug re-engagements (CMU, TRILL, Cassi RossGeske), 9 post re-engagements (Redlands vendor call, Cedaredge coffee shop, Orchard Mesa x4, Two Rivers Winery, Bakery Barn like + comment), 2 within-pass dedups (Unique Solutions Handyman, Sarahdipity), 2 ambiguous-identity fails-closed (Teresa's Plants, AG Property Services).

CAP NOTE: the eligible set is 88 likes + 4 comments, which exceeds the 75-like cap by 13. The pass builder did not enforce the cap before handing to the guard, and the guard does not cap. The worker was already running when this was caught, so it stands for this pass; next pass enforces the 75-like cap at build time. Comments that go out: Jaeger Performance and Detailing, A Keeper of The Cave LLC, Cera Services, Sharp Landscaping (Bakery Barn comment was blocked as re-engagement).

Worker math: 92 actions at 60-120s pacing is roughly 1.5 to 3h. Result file job-20260922-024222.json.result.json expected in the Pig inbox ~22:15-23:45 MDT. Recovery + intent reconciliation + post-pass verify on the next pass via the orphan reaper.

Lessons: (1) in this environment /tmp is not reliable even within a single turn; job files for multi-hour passes now live under ~/workspace/pig-fb/.jobs/. (2) A missing local background session does not mean the remote worker died; verify against the Pig before declaring a dispatch failed.

**Targets:**
- Likes: Aila Thai Kitchen (Delta Applefest post); Smith Family Orchard Store x2 (Dough Pros sourdough; Rocking W milk order); Bodan's Detailing (Kenworth semi detail); Trianna's Fine Designs and Boutique (fall unboxing); EstherJades Ltd (GJ herbal-goods post, like only, author Kathy Wynn personal profile); Morehouse Produce (farm-stand post, like only, author George Morehouse personal profile).

That is not bread, that is a personality trait."); Bodan's Detailing ("A semi interior that clean is basically a unicorn sighting. Respect the elbow grease."); Trianna's Boutique ("Fall unboxings are dangerous for wallets. That lineup looks like trouble in the best way.").

**INFRA INCIDENT + FIX (this pass):** The dispatched worker (PID 49920) died within ~2 min with zero output, no progress file, no result file; a relaunch (PID 10200) died the same way. Root cause proven by isolation test: OpenSSH for Windows places SSH-session processes in a Job Object with KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE; `Start-Process -NoNewWindow` children inherit that job and are killed ~1-2 min after the launching SSH session exits. `Start-Process`-launched sleeper: DEAD at 75s. WMI `Win32_Process.Create`-launched sleeper: ALIVE at 90s+. The orphan-model run_pass.sh (detached launch, returns immediately) has been broken since its introduction; the old synchronous model never hit this because the SSH session stayed open. Pass 113's worker died the same way (its 42 intents sit at EXECUTION with no result; do NOT rerun, collector/orphan reconciliation owns them).
**Fix applied:** run_pass.sh now launches the worker via WMI Win32_Process.Create (surgical edit, verified: guard correctly refused a test dispatch while the worker was in flight). Lesson recorded in AGENTS.md ("Pig detached-launch: use WMI, never Start-Process"). Diagnostic files (fb-diag-zero.*, fb-diag-single.*) cleaned from the Pig inbox.
**Relaunch:** worker relaunched via WMI (PID 42556) at ~14:09 MDT, ran clean to completion.

**Result:** SUCCESS_ACTION. 11/11 ok. 4/4 comments landed ("comment text visible"), 7/7 likes registered ("like registered (button state changed)"). Result file: job-20260924-195021.json.result.json on the Pig. No checkpoint/warning/restriction: volume unchanged, no 14-day hold.
**Collection:** facebook-engagement-collect cron via collect_pass.sh. 6/11 observations recorded at 14:20 MDT (all ok); remaining 5 pending next collector run. Ledger at pass end: 11 intent + 11 execution + 6 observation records; consistent, remainder pending collection.
**Caveat:** Smith Family Orchard Store and Trianna's owner IDs read as business identities but Page-vs-profile type was not independently proven. Verification caveat stands.

**Scheduled:** 2026-09-24 15:26 MDT. **Pass IDs:** fb-20260924-214108 (dispatch 1), fb-20260924-214513 (dispatch 2).
**Discovery:** ~15:28 MDT via fb115-discover.py: 536 candidates across home feeds + local groups, 29 trade-keyword hits. Page timeline fetches returned zero posts.
**Job:** /tmp/fb-job-20260924-213722.json — 22 likes + 5 comments, 27 unique page values, 60–120s pacing. Job-seeking, resale, nonlocal, personal-service, and unsuitable candidates manually removed. Comment targets (business Pages verified, all drafts <20 words, noncommercial, no PING/link/contact): Dough Pros; Smith Family Orchard Store; Rocky Mountain Radon Mitigation; Lil Toot's Party Town; The Bread Basket Farm Stand.
**Guard:** 27 eligible, 0 blocked (21:41 and 21:45 UTC runs).
**Dispatch 1 (fb-20260924-214108):** 27 intents reached EXECUTION; receipt dispatched=false, infra=worker_launch_failed. No engage.js process, no progress/result files. Reconciled: 27 OBSERVATION records, terminal NOT_ATTEMPTED.
**Dispatch 2 (fb-20260924-214513):** same job redispatched (launcher was invoked twice while diagnosing; neither launch executed any Facebook action). 27 intents reached EXECUTION; receipt dispatched=false, infra=worker_launch_failed. Non-execution proven: Pig Win32_Process list shows no engage.js node, inbox holds job-20260924-214513.json with no .result.json and no .progress.jsonl. Reconciled: 27 OBSERVATION records, terminal NOT_ATTEMPTED.
**Zero known executed actions** across both dispatches. No warning, checkpoint, restriction, or login failure observed.
**ROOT CAUSE (proven, supersedes the CLIXML-noise theory recorded on pass 1's observations):** run_pass.sh's WMI launch block had an unterminated PowerShell string. The Create line ended `...`"$job`")` — the backtick escaped the final `"`, so the argument string never closed; PowerShell returned ParserError ("Missing ')' in method call", "The string is missing the terminator") and WMI Create never executed. Both dispatches failed deterministically on this. (Bash heredocs do not process `\"`, which is why the quoting looked right in the file but rendered wrong.)
**Fix applied + validated 2026-09-24 ~16:04 MDT:** added the missing closing quote to the Create line in ~/workspace/pig-fb/run_pass.sh (file still untracked/uncommitted, as before). Validation: exact launcher pipeline against the Pig returned `FBWORKERPID=54420` and the PID parsed cleanly through the run_pass.sh grep chain (VALIDATION-PASS). bash -n clean. No third dispatch was run during this pass per the once-per-pass rule; the next scheduled pass will exercise the fixed launcher for real.
**Result:** NOT_ATTEMPTED. 27 intents × 2 passes, all terminal NOT_ATTEMPTED, zero open records. No goal timeline entry (no meaningful engagement occurred). Infra failure stayed quiet per standing rule.

## Guard patch 2026-09-29 ~06:55 MDT — pfbid blind spot (pass-143 follow-up, code only, no dispatch)
- Root cause confirmed by reproduction (byte-exact: rebuilt the pass-143 pre-filter state — md log truncated pre-pass-144, ledger truncated to the 01:27:03Z build — and reproduced 534 -> 412 fresh with the exact reported tallies ledger-engaged 48 / engaged-name 71 / engaged-post-id 3).
- Missed form: `/posts/pfbid<alphanumeric>` opaque post tokens. The extractor only matched numeric post IDs, so pfbid URLs yielded zero IDs on BOTH sides: 135 terminal ledger intent URLs invisible at pass-143 build time (117 all-time after NOT_ATTEMPTED exclusion), 13 pass-143 candidates invisible, 88 pfbid tokens in the md log unindexed.
- Patch: `FB_PFBID_RE` in engagement-guard.py joins a new shared `FB_URL_ID_RES` tuple used by parse_facebook() (incl. its GUARD-RECORD block) and _fb_post_ids() (check/filter/ledger-merge path). action-intent-ledger.py mirrors the new regex in its _post_ids() tuple. pfbid tokens index as `post:pfbidXXX`, a namespace disjoint from numeric `post:<digits>`. Same post in pfbid form now joins exactly; pfbid-vs-canonical-permalink cross-form remains unjoinable by ID (documented in code; name/recency curation covers it).
- Before/after on pass 143: exclusion sets grew by exactly the previously-invisible pfbid IDs (md post_ids 1321 -> 1409, ledger engaged 2281 -> 2398); pre-filter output unchanged at 534 -> 412 with identical tallies (no pass-143 candidate collides with a terminal pfbid intent, and no numeric-path behavior changed). Synthetic proof: a FAILED terminal pfbid intent URL now classifies ALREADY_ENGAGED by post id (was ELIGIBLE pre-patch); a novel pfbid URL stays ELIGIBLE.
- Tests: test-engagement-guard.sh harness fixed (it copied only 2 of the 5 required logs, so T1-T9 failed at baseline; now copies all five) and extended with T10 pfbid regression (ledger path, md-log path, novel-URL ELIGIBLE). Suite: 22/22 PASS. T10 verified discriminating against a pre-patch guard copy.
- Pre-existing, not touched: 'share'/'reel' slugs in page_slugs (fail-closed false-positive class on share/reel URLs); superseded per-pass build scripts (fb70/fb78/fb84/build-fb110/111) keep their own old regexes but are not in the live path (build-fb-job.py delegates everything to the guard).
